# Skills · G · part 73

923 entries, grill-feature-diagrams to grill-me-codex. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **grill-feature-diagrams**: Elicit a feature exhaustively through a rigorous one-question-at-a-time interview, synthesize an approved feature brief, then delegate creation of a detailed portfolio of editable Excalidraw diagrams to a low-cost model. Use when a user wants to explore, define, visualize, or hand off a product or software feature and needs both deep requirements discovery and architecture, workflow, data, state, sequence, rollout, or operational diagrams. ([M0rtalPhe0nix/dotfiles](https://github.com/M0rtalPhe0nix/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/capability-catalog/grill-feature-diagrams/skill) · ★1)
- **grill-federated**: Clarify a research question or design, test terms and boundaries, and route agreed knowledge through the selected Catalog. Shared by survey and teach; also usable directly. ([impactaky/knowledge-management](https://github.com/impactaky/knowledge-management/tree/HEAD/skills/grill-federated) · ★2)
- **grill-for-implementation**: Grill a concrete implementation plan into pinned implementation decisions in the spec or tickets. ([opengovsg/formsg-ai](https://github.com/opengovsg/formsg-ai/tree/HEAD/skills/engineering/grill-for-implementation) · ★1)
- **grill-for-planning**: Stress-test a vague plan, decision, or idea before it's written down — "grill me on this", "poke holes in this idea", "help me think this through". Round-based interview that maps the idea into a decision tree and works it to a shared understanding. For prepping a specific artifact (RFC/PR/promo packet) against a review panel, use skill-tree:grill-for-quality instead. ([dannybrown37/skill-tree](https://github.com/dannybrown37/skill-tree/tree/HEAD/skills/grill-for-planning))
- **grill-for-poc**: PoC(proof of concept) 착수 전 집요한 인터뷰. 검증할 질문·완료 조건·비교할 대안·범위 밖을 합의해 `/poc`에 넘길 대화 맥락을 만든다. ([ohyecloudy/dotfiles](https://github.com/ohyecloudy/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/claude/skills/grill-for-poc) · ★14)
- **grill-for-quality**: Prep a specific artifact (design doc, PR/diff, promo packet, slides, or an entire codebase) for a real review panel, interview, or presentation — "grill me on this RFC", "quiz me for promo". Adaptive adversarial interview that pushes on real weak points. For stress-testing a vague plan or decision that isn't written down yet, use skill-tree:grill-for-planning instead. ([dannybrown37/skill-tree](https://github.com/dannybrown37/skill-tree/tree/HEAD/skills/grill-for-quality))
- **grill-for-unknowns**: Use only when the user explicitly invokes grill-for-unknowns or asks for a map-vs-territory unknowns pass, blindspot discovery, unknown-known prototypes, or a subagent launch packet before implementation. ([Asymmetric-al/core](https://github.com/Asymmetric-al/core/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/grill-for-unknowns) · ★356 · 4 copies)
- **grill-for-unknowns**: Use when starting or reviewing a complex implementation where the user wants an agent to interrogate the plan against docs/source evidence, surface unknown unknowns, and avoid rushing into build mode. Combines docs-grounded grilling with a map-vs-territory unknowns pass. ([nicobailon/grill-for-unknowns](https://github.com/nicobailon/grill-for-unknowns/tree/HEAD/plugins/grill-for-unknowns) · ★223 · 4 copies)
- **grill-for-visualization**: Turn an idea into a polished coded video (product showcase or system/concept explainer) — "make an animation of how X works", "visualize this for a demo", "grill me for a visualization". Round-based interview about what to show, a storyboard the user approves, then a React project that renders an MP4, with the source kept editable. For stress-testing a plan with no video at the end, use skill-tree:grill-for-planning instead. ([dannybrown37/skill-tree](https://github.com/dannybrown37/skill-tree/tree/HEAD/skills/grill-for-visualization))
- **grill-form**: Use when the user wants to answer a round of grilling/interview/design-review questions through an HTML form instead of chat — "un html avec des inputs pour chaque question", "answer these questions in a form", "copy my answers as a prompt", or when they want to answer a question round asynchronously and hand the answers to another agent session. ([ethansmadjaa/skills](https://github.com/ethansmadjaa/skills/tree/HEAD/grill-form) · ★1)
- **grill-form**: Grill the user relentlessly about a plan, decision, or idea. Use when the user wants to stress-test their thinking, or uses any 'grill' trigger phrases. ([eSAMTrade/skills](https://github.com/eSAMTrade/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/grill-form))
- **grill-founder**: Interview a non-technical founder to surface blind spots before executing any deliverable — code or otherwise. Use before a feature request, a launch, publishing or any external content, marketing copy and positioning decisions, pricing, deploy, hosting, domain or migration work, infrastructure changes, or dispatching agents/subagents to do the building. Trigger on any vague instruction ("add a login page", "refresh the site", "make it feel warmer", "just ship it") and on any request with irreversible side effects or more than an hour of work. ([lightarktech/founder-coding-skills](https://github.com/lightarktech/founder-coding-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/grill-founder) · ★1)
- **grill-from-ticktick**: Take a grilling session that is running over TickTick back into the terminal — ingest whatever the user answered on the phone, retire the rest, and continue the interview live. Use when the user says "let's finish this here", "bring the grill back", "continue in the terminal", or invokes /grill-from-ticktick. ([bashycodes/marketplace](https://github.com/bashycodes/marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/bashy-plugins/skills/grill-from-ticktick))
- **grill-full**: Strictest form of grill: ask about every open point, even small ones, pick no defaults on your own, flag contradictions, and confirm a full summary before starting. Invoke only with /rs:grill-full. Stays on until the user says "stop grill" or "normal mode". ([reza-sanjari/rs-toolbox](https://github.com/reza-sanjari/rs-toolbox/tree/HEAD/plugins/rs/skills/grill-full))
- **grill-gaps**: Use when open ontology gaps block a decision. Asks one focused question at a time, with a recommended answer and a fixed question budget. ([Vladick-Pick/business-ontology](https://github.com/Vladick-Pick/business-ontology/tree/HEAD/skills/grill-gaps) · ★3)
- **grill-goal**: Clarify the user's intent through an in-depth dialogue, then write a standalone, verifiable Markdown goal for another AI. Use to define, write, or improve a goal without executing the work it describes. ([bacoco/ShipGuard](https://github.com/bacoco/ShipGuard/tree/HEAD/plugins/shipguard/skills/grill-goal) · ★7)
- **grill-graph**: Relentless one-question-at-a-time design grilling — the same interrogation core as grill-me — that also preserves the decision's evolution as a graph: rejected branches and why, back-references to earlier decisions, and re-assumptions. Each resolved decision is held in context, then the whole graph is written and rendered once, at convergence, so nothing ever slows a question. Use this instead of grill-me whenever a design discussion is likely to branch or to reopen earlier decisions and you want the decision topology preserved, then converged into spec.md. For a quick throwaway linear stress… ([LeaYeh/skills](https://github.com/LeaYeh/skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/grill-graph/skills/grill-graph) · ★2)
- **grill-harness**: Adversarial grilling harness that extends upstream /grilling with an expert panel, structured decision output, and enhanced CONTEXT.md format. Scans ~/.claude/agents/ and ~/.config/opencode/agent/ for available expert personas and assembles a challenge panel. Composes with /grilling, /grill-with-docs, /grill-me, and /wayfinder sessions that invoke grilling. Use when user says /grill-harness, /grilling, /grill-with-docs, /grill-me, "grill with experts", "adversarial review", "challenge this with a panel", or wants structured decision tables from grilling. ([vvaldez/agent-skills](https://github.com/vvaldez/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/skill-extras/skills/grill-harness))
- **grill-harness-with-docs**: Grounds agent work in authoritative evidence, establishes shared understanding, critiques resolved material decisions and changes with a fresh agent, and resolves genuinely open decisions with a human. Use for resolved material work needing independent critique or for any product, domain, engineering, architecture, operations, security, compliance, or harness topic with unresolved intent, semantics, authority, consequential trade-offs, or material risk. ([ldaume/agentic-engineering-harness](https://github.com/ldaume/agentic-engineering-harness/tree/HEAD/skills/engineering/grill-harness-with-docs) · ★3)
- **grill-idea**: Interview the user relentlessly about a refined idea until shared decisions, assumptions, and blockers are clear enough for a spec. ([emanualjade/strike](https://github.com/emanualjade/strike/tree/HEAD/plugins/strike/skills/grill-idea))
- **grill-idea**: Challenge a product or feature idea before any code. Use before vibe-setup, new apps, or large features when the request is fuzzy, oversized, or “build me X” without constraints. Asks hard one-at-a-time questions and produces a tight build brief. ([Jaysi88/agent-skills](https://github.com/Jaysi88/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/grill-idea))
- **grill-implement-parallel**: Orquestra os workers não-frontier da etapa implement-parallel sobre o Execution DAG, com worktree isolado por worker e receipt submetido apenas pelo leader. ([cadugevaerd/grill-with-docs](https://github.com/cadugevaerd/grill-with-docs/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/grill-implement-parallel))
- **grill-implementation**: 実装方針の未決事項を対話で整理する。要求が不明でユーザー固有の判断が残るとき、または明示的なインタビュー依頼時に使う。確定事項や routine details は再確認しない。 ([harunonsystem/harunon-harness-slim](https://github.com/harunonsystem/harunon-harness-slim/tree/HEAD/claude/skills/grill-implementation) · 2 copies)
- **grill-ingest**: Ingest documents (PDF, DOCX, PPTX, XLSX, HTML, Markdown, plain text, images) into the POMA Grill context engine so they become semantically searchable. Use when the user says "grill my &lt;file&gt;", or asks to ingest, index, upload, or add a document to POMA Grill, or wants a PDF/report/contract made searchable. Also covers batch ingestion, job status polling, and resuming an in-progress ingest. ([poma-ai/poma-grill-mcp](https://github.com/poma-ai/poma-grill-mcp/tree/HEAD/plugins/poma-grill/skills/grill-ingest))
- **grill-initiative**: A relentless interview to sharpen a Metis initiative, writing settled answers into the initiative document as you go and recording hard-to-reverse trade-offs as ADRs. Use when the user wants to create, challenge, or design an initiative before it is decomposed. Pass an initiative short code (e.g. PROJ-I-0001) or a title for a new initiative. ([colliery-io/metis](https://github.com/colliery-io/metis/tree/HEAD/plugins/metis/skills/grill-initiative) · ★15)
- **grill-initiative**: A relentless interview that sharpens a Kairos initiative (or a strategy) and writes each settled answer into the item at once. Pass an initiative or strategy short code, or a title for a new initiative. ([colliery-io/kairos](https://github.com/colliery-io/kairos/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/workflow/grill-initiative) · ★1)
- **grill-intent**: Adversarial review of an existing intent spec. Walk the spec field-by-field and pressure-test the weakest claims before code gets written against them. Use when an intent feels "done" but the user wants to stress-test it, or before handing it to an implementation agent. ([pathmodeio/claude-plugin](https://github.com/pathmodeio/claude-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/grill-intent) · ★3 · 3 copies)
- **grill-interview**: Interactive design-tree interview protocol for cognitive drift prevention, clarifying ambiguities, and synthesizing CONTEXT.md before implementation. ([kpango/dotfiles](https://github.com/kpango/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/agent/skills/grill-interview) · ★22)
- **grill-interview-architect**: Socratic interview architect for techinterview.live. - **grill-into-design**: Use when starting a non-trivial design decision before any code lands — feature design, architecture choices, spec'ing out a system. Triggers on "design a feature", "design X", "brainstorm X", "let's brainstorm", "spec out Y", "write a spec for Z", "plan a feature", "grill me on this", "stress-test this idea", "interrogate me on this plan", "let's figure out X together", "what should we build for Y", or "how should I approach this" on a design problem. Prefer over superpowers:brainstorming when both could fire. Not for single-step tasks, bug fixes with a known root cause, or pure exploration… ([xinye1/xl-skills](https://github.com/xinye1/xl-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/grill-into-design))
- **grill-issue**: issue を 1 件 grill して受け入れ条件・触ってよいファイル・確認コマンド・決定事項を本文へ書き戻し、agent-ready かどうかを判定してラベルを付け替える。夜間ランナー (`agent-ready` を拾う側) の入力を作る skill。issue に紐づかない設計相談は /grill-me を使う ([ymnao/dotfiles](https://github.com/ymnao/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/claude/skills/grill-issue) · ★1)
- **grill-issue**: GitHub issue を grilling（質問攻めの対話）で詰めて、spec に落とせる状態まで鋭くするスキル。 既存 issue を詰める（/spec-intake:grill-issue &lt;番号&gt;）ことも、issue が無い状態から詰めて最後に 新規作成する（/spec-intake:grill-issue）こともできる。詰め終わった issue は /spec-intake:spec-draft で spec 化する。 手動起動: /spec-intake:grill-issue [issue番号]。 ([MacoTasu/agent-skills](https://github.com/MacoTasu/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/spec-intake/skills/grill-issue))

- **grill-me**: Structured interrogation skill for stress-testing plans, designs, and ideas. Use when the user says "grill me", "grill me about", "stress test my plan", or "poke holes in this", or asks to resume an earlier grill session. - **grill-me**: Knowledge-extraction interview. EXTRACTS knowledge FROM Alex — NOT a quiz that tests Alex, and NOT /roast (which attacks a plan). ([cacardinal/laila](https://github.com/cacardinal/laila/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/grill-me) · ★1)
